where the last inequality again follows from the gap assumption. Since

\Delta_{\mathcal{J}}^{\Phi}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})\geq\delta_{k}/2
, we get

\|Q_{\mathcal{J}}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}}\|\leq\frac{1}{10}\left(8+\frac{5}{3}\right)\frac{\Delta_{\mathcal{J}}^{\Phi}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})}{\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}}.

Returning to (
H.1
), we obtain

s_{\min}(L_{{\mathcal{J}},s})\geq\frac{1}{30}\frac{\Delta_{J}^{\Phi}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})}{\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}}\geq\frac{1}{60}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}}.

Thus
\|L_{{\mathcal{J}},s}^{-1}\|\leq 60\frac{\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}}{\delta_{k}}
. This proves the desired bound.

H.2
Proof of Proposition
3.1

For
s\in[k]
, the eigen-equation gives

\widetilde{u}_{s}=G(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})A\widetilde{u}_{s}=\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})A\widetilde{u}_{s}+\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})A\widetilde{u}_{s},

where
\Xi(z):=G(z)-\Phi(z).
It follows that

U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}=U^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}.

Taking the
\mathcal{J}
-block yields

\displaystyle U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}=U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{I}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{I}}\cdot U_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}}\cdot U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}.

Collecting the
U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}
terms gives

(I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}})U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}=U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{I}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{I}}\cdot U_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}.

Recall
\mathcal{T}_{s}
from (
3.1
). We further get

\displaystyle U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}-\mathcal{T}_{s}U_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}=(I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}})^{-1}U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}.

(H.3)

By definition and
\mathcal{N}=\{r_{+}+1,\dots,r\}
,

P_{U}\widetilde{u}_{s}=U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U_{\mathcal{J}}U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U_{\mathcal{N}}U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}.

Combining this with (
H.3
), we get

\displaystyle w_{s}^{\mathrm{orc}}

\displaystyle=P_{U}\widetilde{u}_{s}-U_{\mathcal{J}}{\mathcal{T}}_{s}U_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}

\displaystyle=U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U_{\mathcal{N}}U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}+U_{\mathcal{J}}(I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}})^{-1}U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\cdot\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}.

(H.4)

Note that we have established in (
7.8
) that

\displaystyle\|U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|<4\frac{\|\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{N}\mathcal{K}}\|}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}+24\frac{R_{\max}}{{\lambda}_{k}^{4}}\mathrm{osc}(\mathcal{R})+4\frac{M}{\lambda_{k}}.

(H.5)

Now we estimate the last term on the right-hand side of (
H.2
). We first have

\|\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|\leq 2|\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}|.

To see this, from
(A+E)\widetilde{u}_{s}=\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}\widetilde{u}_{s},
we have

A\widetilde{u}_{s}=(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}I-E)\widetilde{u}_{s}.

Multiplying by
U^{\mathrm{T}}
gives
\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}=U^{\mathrm{T}}A\widetilde{u}_{s}=U^{\mathrm{T}}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}I-E)\widetilde{u}_{s}.
Hence,

\|\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|\leq|\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}|+\|E\|\leq 2|\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}|

by our assumption
\lambda_{k}\geq 9\sqrt{R_{\max}}
and Weyl’s inequality.

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{D}
, we have

\|U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\|\leq\frac{M}{\widetilde{\lambda}_{s}^{2}}.

Combining these estimates with Lemma
3.1
yields

\displaystyle\|U_{\mathcal{J}}(I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}})^{-1}U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\cdot\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\|(I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}})^{-1}U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\cdot\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|\leq 120\frac{M}{\delta_{k}}.

(H.6)

From (
H.2
) and the orthogonality of the signal subspaces,
w_{s}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}
lies entirely in the span of
U_{k}^{\perp}
. Combining the above bounds, we have

\displaystyle\|w_{s}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|+\|U_{\mathcal{J}}(I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}})^{-1}U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\cdot\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\|\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{N}\mathcal{K}}\|}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}+24\frac{R_{\max}}{{\lambda}_{k}^{4}}\mathrm{osc}(\mathcal{R})+124\frac{M}{\delta_{k}}.

Denote

\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}:=(w_{1}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{1},\ldots,w_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{k}).

It follows immediately that

\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|\leq\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{k}\left(4\frac{\|\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{N}\mathcal{K}}\|}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}+24\frac{R_{\max}}{{\lambda}_{k}^{4}}\mathrm{osc}(\mathcal{R})+124\frac{M}{\delta_{k}}\right)=\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

We rewrite

W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=(w_{1}^{\mathrm{orc}},\ldots,w_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})=U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}+\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}.

Note that

\displaystyle U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=\orth(W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})=W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}((W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{\mathrm{T}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{-1/2}.

(H.7)

We aim to bound

\|\sin\angle(U_{k},U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})\|=\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|=\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}((W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{\mathrm{T}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{-1/2}\|\leq\frac{\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|}{s_{\min}(W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})}.

First,

\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|=\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}(U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}+\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}})\|=\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|\leq\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|.

Next, since
W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}+\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}
, and
\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}
is orthogonal to
U_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}(W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\min}(U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k})=\sigma_{\min}(U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k})=\sqrt{1-\|\sin\angle(U_{k},\widetilde{U}_{k})\|^{2}}.

On the event that Theorem
3
holds,
\|\sin\angle(U_{k},\widetilde{U}_{k})\|\leq 1/100
. Thus,

\sigma_{\min}(W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})\geq\frac{1}{2}.

We conclude that

\|\sin\angle(U_{k},U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})\|\leq 2\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|\leq 2\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

For the
2\to\infty
norm bound, we start with the standard decomposition

\displaystyle\min_{O\in\mathbb{O}(k)}\|U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}O\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|(I-P_{U_{k}})U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}+\|U_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\|\sin\angle(U_{k},U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})\|^{2}.

It remains to bound
\|(I-P_{U_{k}})U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}
. Since
P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}
, from (
H.7
), we get

(I-P_{U_{k}})U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}((W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{\mathrm{T}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{-1/2}.

Thus

\displaystyle\|(I-P_{U_{k}})U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}\|((W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{\mathrm{T}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})^{-1/2}\|

(H.8)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}}{\sigma_{\min}(W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})}\leq 2\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}.

(H.9)

We bound
\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}
. From (
H.2
),

\displaystyle\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}

\displaystyle=(w_{1}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{1},\ldots,w_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{k})

\displaystyle=U_{\mathcal{N}}\cdot U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}+U_{\mathcal{J}}\cdot\mathtt{B}

where
\mathtt{B}:=[\mathtt{b_{1},\cdots,b_{k}}]
with columns

\mathtt{b}_{s}=(I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}})^{-1}U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Xi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U\cdot\Lambda U^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}.

In (
H.2
), we have

\|\mathtt{b}_{s}\|\leq 120\frac{M}{\delta_{k}}

and thus

\|\mathtt{B}\|\leq\|\mathtt{B}\|_{F}\leq 120\sqrt{k}\frac{M}{\delta_{k}}.

Also, by (
H.5
), we get

\displaystyle\|U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}\|\leq\|U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k}\max_{s\in k}\|U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{k}\frac{\|\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{N}\mathcal{K}}\|}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}+24\sqrt{k}\frac{R_{\max}}{{\lambda}_{k}^{4}}\mathrm{osc}(\mathcal{R})+4\sqrt{k}\frac{M}{\lambda_{k}}.

Now we get

\displaystyle\|(I-P_{U_{k}})U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}=2\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|e_{i}^{\mathrm{T}}(U_{\mathcal{N}}\cdot U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}+U_{\mathcal{J}}\cdot\mathtt{B})\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|U\|_{2,\infty}(\|U_{\mathcal{N}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}\|+\|\mathtt{B}\|)

\displaystyle\leq\|U\|_{2,\infty}\left(8\sqrt{k}\frac{\|\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{N}\mathcal{K}}\|}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}+48\sqrt{k}\frac{R_{\max}}{{\lambda}_{k}^{4}}\mathrm{osc}(\mathcal{R})+248\sqrt{k}\frac{M}{\delta_{k}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\|U\|_{2,\infty}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

Finally, combining the bound on
\|\sin\angle(U_{k},U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})\|
, we obtain

\displaystyle\min_{O\in\mathbb{O}(k)}\|U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}-U_{k}O\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|(I-P_{U_{k}})U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}+\|U_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\|\sin\angle(U_{k},U_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|U\|_{2,\infty}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

The proof is complete.

H.3
Proof of (
I.4
) and (
I
)

By the definitions of
\widehat{w}_{s}
and
w_{s}^{\mathrm{orc}}
, their difference is

\widehat{w}_{s}-w_{s}^{\mathrm{orc}}=P_{\widetilde{U}}\widetilde{u}_{s}-P_{U}\widetilde{u}_{s}-\left(\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{s}\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}-U_{\mathcal{J}}\mathcal{T}_{s}U_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\right).

Since
P_{\widetilde{U}}\widetilde{u}_{s}=\widetilde{u}_{s}
, we get

\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=(I-P_{U})\widetilde{U}_{k}-\mathfrak{D}_{k}.

Let
\Delta:=\|\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|
. Applying the triangle inequality, we bound

\Delta\leq\|(I-P_{U})\widetilde{U}_{k}\|+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|\leq 2\frac{\|E\|}{\lambda_{k}}+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|.

(H.10)

The bound in the second inequality was established in (
B.2
).

Similarly, taking the
2\to\infty
norm yields

\Delta_{2,\infty}:=\|\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|(I-P_{U})\widetilde{U}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}.

(H.11)

Note that the assumption
\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\frac{\|E\|}{\lambda_{k}}+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|\leq c
guarantees that
\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\Delta\leq c\leq 1/10
for a sufficiently small absolute constant
c
.

Recall from the proof of Proposition
3.1
that
W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=U_{k}U_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{U}_{k}+\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}
, where
\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|\leq\|\Delta^{\mathrm{orc}}\|\leq\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}
and
\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|U\|_{2,\infty}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}
.

From the decomposition
\widehat{W}_{k}=W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+(\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})
, we obtain

\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}\widehat{W}_{k}\|\leq\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|+\|\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|\leq\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\Delta.

(H.12)

Similarly, the
2\to\infty
norm satisfies

\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}\widehat{W}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|U\|_{2,\infty}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\Delta_{2,\infty}.

(H.13)

Next, we lower-bound
s_{\min}(\widehat{W}_{k})
. We established previously that
s_{\min}(W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})\geq 1/2
. By Weyl’s inequality,

s_{\min}(\widehat{W}_{k})\geq s_{\min}(W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}})-\|\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}\|\geq\frac{1}{2}-\Delta\geq\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{10}=\frac{2}{5}

since
\Delta\leq 1/10
.

Similar to the proof of Proposition
3.1
, we get

\|\sin\angle(U_{k},\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}})\|=\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}\|\leq\frac{\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}\widehat{W}_{k}\|}{\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{W}_{k})}\leq\frac{5}{2}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\Delta).

For the
2\to\infty
bound, we start with

\min_{O\in\mathbb{O}(k)}\|\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}-U_{k}O\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|(I-P_{U_{k}})\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}\|_{2,\infty}+\|U_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\|\sin\angle(U_{k},\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}})\|^{2}.

Since
(I-P_{U_{k}})\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}=P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}\widehat{W}_{k}(\widehat{W}_{k}^{\mathrm{T}}\widehat{W}_{k})^{-1/2}
, we bound the first term using (
H.13
):

\|(I-P_{U_{k}})\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{\|P_{U_{k}^{\perp}}\widehat{W}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}}{\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{W}_{k})}\leq\frac{5}{2}\left(\|U\|_{2,\infty}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\Delta_{2,\infty}\right).

Substituting this and the bound on
\|\sin\angle(U_{k},\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}})\|^{2}
back into the decomposition yields the desired bound.

Appendix I
Plug-in de-biasing implementation and limitations

To keep the discussion simple, assume in this paragraph that
r
and
r_{+}
are known and the variance profile
\Sigma
is known, so that

\Phi(z)
is computable from the QVE..

Define

\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}:=(\widetilde{u}_{k+1},\ldots,\widetilde{u}_{r_{+}}),\qquad\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{I}}:=(\widetilde{u}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{u}_{k},\widetilde{u}_{r_{+}+1},\ldots,\widetilde{u}_{r}),

with corresponding diagonal eigenvalue matrices
\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\mathcal{J}}

and
\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\mathcal{I}}
. Also write

\widetilde{U}:=(\widetilde{u}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{u}_{r}),\qquad P_{\widetilde{U}}:=\widetilde{U}\widetilde{U}^{\mathrm{T}}.

If
\mathcal{J}=\emptyset
, no correction is needed and we set
\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}:=\widetilde{U}_{k}
. Otherwise, for
s\in[k]
, define

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{s}:=\left(I-\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\mathcal{J}}\right)^{-1}\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{I}}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{\mathcal{I}},

(I.1)

whenever the inverse exists. The plug-in corrected vector is

\widehat{w}_{s}:=P_{\widetilde{U}}\widetilde{u}_{s}-\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{s}\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s},\qquad s=1,\ldots,k.

(I.2)

Note that for
s\leq k
,
P_{\widetilde{U}}\widetilde{u}_{s}=\widetilde{u}_{s}
. Finally, set

\widehat{W}_{k}:=(\widehat{w}_{1},\ldots,\widehat{w}_{k}),\qquad\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}:=\orth(\widehat{W}_{k}).

To compare this estimator with the oracle estimator, define the correction discrepancy

\mathfrak{D}_{k}:=\left(\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{s}\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}-U_{\mathcal{J}}\mathcal{T}_{s}U_{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{T}}\widetilde{u}_{s}\right)_{s=1}^{k}.

Then the difference between the plug-in and oracle matrices is given by

\displaystyle\widehat{W}_{k}-W_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}=(I-P_{U})\widetilde{U}_{k}-\mathfrak{D}_{k}.

(I.3)

Consequently, the accuracy of the plug-in correction is governed by the norms of this difference. As shown in (
B.2
) below,
\|(I-P_{U})\widetilde{U}_{k}\|\leq 2\|E\|/\lambda_{k}
. On the event that

\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\frac{\|E\|}{\lambda_{k}}+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|\leq c

for a sufficiently small absolute constant
c>0
, the same
argument as in Proposition
3.1

gives

\|\sin\angle(\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}},U_{k})\|\leq C\left(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\frac{\|E\|}{\lambda_{k}}+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|\right).

(I.4)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\min_{O\in\mathbb{O}(k)}\|\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}-U_{k}O\|_{2,\infty}\leq C\Big[

\displaystyle\|U\|_{2,\infty}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\|(I-P_{U})\widetilde{U}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle+\|U_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\left(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\frac{\|E\|}{\lambda_{k}}+\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|\right)^{2}\Big].

(I.5)

The proofs of (
I.4
) and (
I
) are given in Appendix
H.3
for completeness.

R
EMARK
I.1

(Limitation and useful regimes)
.

The plug-in correction
\widehat{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{db}}
is not a universal replacement for the raw empirical eigenspace
\widetilde{U}_{k}
. The term
\mathfrak{D}_{k}
measures the error in estimating the oracle correction from the empirical lower-positive
\mathcal{J}
-block. This term could be large in two situations. First, if the lower-positive block
\mathcal{J}
contains weak or poorly
separated spikes, then the empirical block

\widetilde{U}_{\mathcal{J}}
may not estimate
U_{\mathcal{J}}
accurately. Second, when eigenvalues are tightly clustered (
\delta_{k}
is small), the matrices
I-U_{\mathcal{J}}^{\mathrm{T}}\Phi(\widetilde{\lambda}_{s})U_{\mathcal{J}}\Lambda_{\mathcal{J}}
become ill-conditioned. Inverting them will make
\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|
blow up.

Thus the plug-in estimator is useful only when

\|\mathfrak{D}_{k}\|\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}

and the removed bias is larger than the residual terms

\frac{\sqrt{k}}{\delta_{k}\lambda_{k}}\left(\|\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{J}\mathcal{I}}\|+\frac{R_{\max}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}\mathrm{osc}(\mathcal{R})\right)\gg\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\mathrm{orc}}+\frac{\|E\|}{\lambda_{k}}.

This condition does not require the geometric bias to vanish. It may hold, for instance, when
\mathcal{J}
contains strong, well-separated spikes that can be accurately estimated, even if the variance profile
\mathcal{R}
has a large coupling between
U_{\mathcal{J}}
and
U_{\mathcal{I}}
. A systematic analysis of optimal fully data-driven
bias correction is left for future work.

Ke Wang is supported by Hong Kong RGC 16304222.
